\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{Nb6}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Nb6} (\texttt{d7b6}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0026\#3023} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=80, QAP obj=32]: the master played \texttt{Bf3}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nd2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Be2} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{P}c6 as their \emph{N}b1, our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{R}h1; transferred move \texttt{d7b6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0026\#3158} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=81, QAP obj=44]: the master played \texttt{Bc4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bf1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bf1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{B}c8 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{N}f6 as their \emph{Q}d1; transferred move \texttt{f8c5}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0022\#762} (ply 21) [structural distance $d$=79, QAP obj=31]: the master played \texttt{h5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pg2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ph2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{P}c6 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{N}d7 as their \emph{Q}d8; transferred move \texttt{g7g5}. Synthesis: 1 retrieved analog transferred to \texttt{Nb6} (summed QAP objective 32, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
\end{figure}
